# Lula convoca militância para pressionar STF contra bets

**BRASIL**

*Governo busca mobilizar apoiadores antes de julgamento no Supremo sobre proibição das apostas.*

Edição do Meio-dia · terça-feira, 3 de novembro de 2026 · período 06h00 → 11h00 (Brasília) · 711 notícias de 64 fontes

![Lula convoca militância para pressionar STF contra bets](<https://f.i.uol.com.br/fotografia/2026/09/01/17882953846a9738d830c2c_1788295384_3x2_rt.jpg>)

::: info Cobertura
622 notícias representadas de 622 elegíveis.
:::

O presidente Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva convocou a militância nas redes sociais para pressionar o STF contra as bets.

A mobilização acontece antes de audiência pública no Supremo para discutir ações sobre o mercado de apostas.

Em setembro, Lula assinou medida provisória que proíbe a atuação das bets no Brasil.

> Lula convoca militância nas redes sociais para pressionar STF contra bets
>
> — Folha de S.Paulo

*Fontes: [Folha de S.Paulo](<https://c-level.folha.uol.com.br/negocios/2026/11/lula-convoca-militancia-nas-redes-sociais-para-pressionar-stf-contra-bets.shtml>) · [Metrópoles](<https://www.metropoles.com/brasil/antes-de-audiencia-no-stf-lula-pede-pressao-da-sociedade-civil-contra-bets>) · [Poder360](<https://www.poder360.com.br/poder-justica/ao-vivo-stf-discute-fim-das-bets-em-audiencia-publica/>)*

### json.dumps Writes NaN by Default: 31 LLM Reports JSON.parse Rejected

### China puxa travão de mão às entradas em bolsa para tentar conter queda dos mercados

### GAVAGAI: Referential Alignment for Infant-Scale Vision-Language Models via Balanced Optimal Transport

A child learns which words name which things from roughly 100 million words of input.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00319>)*

### Queering the Audits: Community-Based Auditing of AI Harms to Queer Communities

AI systems embed majority-group defaults into training data, evaluation metrics, and category definitions, producing documented harms for queer communities including erasure, misclassification, and discrimination.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00345>)*

### Cross-Prompt Reuse of Entity-Replacement Responses

With model weights fixed, we measure how much of an entity-replacement response transfers between English factual questions, beyond whether answers change.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00352>)*

### Humanized but Still True? Auditing Facts and Detection in AI-Text Humanizers

AI "humanizers" rewrite machine-generated text so that detectors classify it as human-written. Public leaderboards score evasion and embedding similarity, but not whether checkable details survive.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00359>)*

### Circuit Oracle: Automating Attribution Graph Analysis via Natural-Language Queries

Attribution graphs, an emerging tool in mechanistic interpretability, use transcoders to decompose language model computations into sparse interpretable features connected by causal edges.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00420>)*

### Fidelity Collapses with Table Size, Not Turn Count: Delivery Architecture in a Deployed Tool-Using Agent

When a multi-turn agent delivers a tool's structured records into the model's context and re-emits them each turn, every turn can corrupt data the tool computed correctly.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00442>)*

### From Lookups to Lessons: Turning Retrieved Knowledge into Parametric Memory

Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) helps large language models (LLMs) answer factual questions beyond their training data, but it adds storage and inference costs.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00450>)*

### Steering Readerly Dynamics: Trajectory-Guided Story Generation with Large Language Models

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate stories, but these stories struggle to reproduce engaging readerly dynamics, the evolving reader responses that shape narrative experience.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00469>)*

### Spotting the Artificial: Dialogic Humor Improvisation in Humans and LLMs

We propose a methodology for investigating the differences between natural and synthetic language in a challenging setting: humorous improvisational dialogue.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00492>)*

### AStockArena: Beyond Returns, Can We Entrust Real Capital to the Decisions of Financial Agents?

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in dynamic financial trading, yet real-time trading benchmarks mainly evaluate agents by cumulative returns and risk-adjusted performance.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00515>)*

### Dual-Axis Policy Entailment: A Benchmark for Policy-Change Reasoning

Whether a policy shifts domains and, by extension, continues, broadens, narrows, or reverses previous policy is of paramount importance to the study of public policy.

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00536>)*

### Distinguishing among reasoning levels of GPT 5.5 Pro via a grammar lens benchmark

Does the level of test-time processing effort affect a language model's computational capability?

*Fontes: [cs.CL updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00555>)*
